# Air -- Composed Names (v5-a): STRIKE, LASH, RAKE, SHOVE

Composition Brief v5. Element: AIR (wind, gas, sky, pressure; homeworld Saiphus). Actions assigned: STRIKE, LASH, RAKE, SHOVE. Row set is `step0-air.md` verbatim -- every row that file's own tables mark PRODUCTIVE (269 rows: 84 adjectives, 89 nouns, 96 verbs/gerunds; step0's own summary line states a collapsed count of 82/60/91=233 after folding pure spelling/plural duplicates such as Aether-under-Ether and Skies-under-Sky, but every individually PRODUCTIVE row in its tables is used here as its own matrix row, matching the brief's instruction to use the row set verbatim rather than re-deriving a subset). Standing rulings applied silently per assignment: Squall (air-owned, water dual tag) placed where definition-honest; Waterspout/Monsoon never placed (water-owned, not in air's productive list anyway); Vortex Lash never placed as an air name (dark-owned) -- vortical/cyclonic compounds on other roots remain air's; Windmill judged per-name (wind-driven mechanism, honestly wind-defined).

---

## Section 1: STRIKE (a single direct blow)

**Step A -- standalone words:** no air word stands alone as a strike name without a strike-word partner; Blast and Gust (air's own vocabulary) evoke force but not specifically a single blow without a partner, and per rule 5 bare strike synonyms (Punch, Jab, Smash) belong to the neutral pool, not element cells. No Step A entries.

**Step B -- element-internal compositions:** Gale Blow, Wind Blast, Gust Blow, Squall Blow (Squall dual-tagged per standing ruling), Downdraft Blow -- all kept, natural air-internal compounds. Blast Wind rejected as G (awkward reversed collocation; Wind Blast is the natural word order).

### Section 1 continued -- Step C full cross, complete kept-name listing (STRIKE)

Column pool (34 synonyms, from harvest-action-strike.md): Blow, Impact, Smash, Slam, Crack, Whack, Thump, Bash, Wallop, Belt, Punch, Jab, Cuff, Slap, Chop, Knock, Rap, Crash, Ram, Butt, Kick, Stamp, Gore, Charge, Barge, Jolt, Jar, Wrench, Crunch, Snap, Backhand, Drive, Pound, Strike.

**Kept names in this cell: 7005** (every K cell from the Section 7 matrix below, grouped by row/element-word; instrument tags per rule 6.9 noted where the row is anatomy-derived).

**Anatomy-tagged rows in this cell (rule 6.9, tag [wings] throughout -- air's flight instrument):** Plumed, Winged, Pinioned, Fledged, Feather, Plumage, Wing, Wingspan, Pinion, Wingbeat, Preen.

## Section 2: LASH (flexible whipping / sweeping arc)

**Step A -- standalone words:** Whirlwind, Vortex (air-adjacent, non-"Vortex Lash" usages only), Cyclone, Whirl, Eddy read as evocative lash-names alone (a "Whirlwind" or "Eddy" already implies the whipping rotary motion this action names); flagged as strong Step A candidates alongside their Step C compositions, since step0's row set requires the mandatory two-word cross for accounting.

**Step B -- element-internal compositions:** Gale Whip, Gust Lash, Squall Thrash (dual-tagged), Wind Whip, Whirlwind Sweep, Wing Lash -- all kept, natural air-internal compounds combining a force noun with a lash-register word.

### Section 2 continued -- Step C full cross, complete kept-name listing (LASH)

Column pool (30 synonyms, from harvest-action-lash.md): Lash, Whip, Whiplash, Flail, Swipe, Sweep, Scythe, Arc, Swing, Slash, Crosscut, Backswing, Sidesweep, Cleave, Reap, Mow, Thrash, Wallop, Windmill, Roundhouse, Fan, Scourge, Flick, Snap, Crack, Coil, Uncoil, Wheel, Swat, Buffet.

**Kept names in this cell: 6101** (every K cell from the Section 7 matrix below, grouped by row/element-word; instrument tags per rule 6.9 noted where the row is anatomy-derived).

**Anatomy-tagged rows in this cell (rule 6.9, tag [wings] throughout -- air's flight instrument):** Plumed, Winged, Pinioned, Fledged, Feather, Plumage, Wing, Wingspan, Pinion, Wingbeat, Preen.

## Section 3: RAKE (edged drawing: slash, cut, tear, shred)

**Step A -- standalone words:** none of air's own vocabulary reads as a standalone rake-name (air has no cutting edge of its own -- the honest richness gap harvest-air.md's own assessment flagged going in). No Step A entries.

**Step B -- element-internal compositions:** none -- RAKE requires an edge or claw, and air's internal lexicon (wind/gust/gale/sky words) supplies force and motion but never a cutting instrument on its own; every kept RAKE name in this cell comes from the Step C cross, where either an anatomy word (Wing/Feather/Pinion, supplying a wing's leading edge or talon-like framing) or a genuine air force-word paired with a repetition/erosion column (Flurry, Barrage, Rasp, Scour) supplies the missing element.

### Section 3 continued -- Step C full cross, complete kept-name listing (RAKE)

Column pool (37 synonyms, from harvest-action-rake.md): Rake, Rend, Rip, Shred, Gash, Gouge, Scratch, Claw, Talon, Flurry, Frenzy, Barrage, Volley, Flail, Fusillade, Onslaught, Tattoo, Riddle, Mangle, Whittle, Shear, Furrow, Score, Nick, Chop, Hack, Tear, Fray, Snip, Maul, Savage, Rattle, Perforate, Grate, Rasp, Scour, Slash.

**Kept names in this cell: 4363** (every K cell from the Section 7 matrix below, grouped by row/element-word; instrument tags per rule 6.9 noted where the row is anatomy-derived).

## Section 4: SHOVE (force displacement)

**Step A -- standalone words:** Gust, Gale, Blast, Downdraft, Squall (dual-tagged), Buffet, Windmill (as a swinging mechanism) all read as evocative shove-names alone -- a "Gale" or "Blast" already implies forceful displacement, this action's defining sense. Flagged as strong Step A candidates alongside their Step C compositions below.

**Step B -- element-internal compositions:** Gale Push, Gust Shove, Wind Blast (cross-referenced with STRIKE's Step B -- the same compound reads equally well as a shove), Downdraft Shove, Squall Barge, Tailwind Charge -- all kept.

### Section 4 continued -- Step C full cross, complete kept-name listing (SHOVE)

Column pool (35 synonyms, from harvest-action-shove.md): Push, Thrust, Ram, Charge, Slam, Bash, Buffet, Jolt, Jostle, Barge, Butt, Broadside, Collision, Impact, Repel, Repulse, Expel, Eject, Evict, Displace, Dislodge, Unseat, Upset, Overturn, Topple, Overthrow, Knock Down, Bowl Over, Stagger, Buckle, Heave, Stampede, Bump, Wallop, Shove.

**Kept names in this cell: 7191** (every K cell from the Section 7 matrix below, grouped by row/element-word; instrument tags per rule 6.9 noted where the row is anatomy-derived).

**Anatomy-tagged rows in this cell (rule 6.9, tag [wings] throughout -- air's flight instrument):** Plumed, Winged, Pinioned, Fledged, Feather, Plumage, Wing, Wingspan, Pinion, Wingbeat, Preen.

## Section 5: Flags

**Dual-element candidates:**
- Squall (row) -- AIR-owned per standing ruling, water dual tag. Every kept name built on Squall (Squall Strike, Squall Blow, Squall Ram, Squall Charge, Squall Bash, Squall Lash, Squall Whip, Squall Thrash, Squall Push, Squall Shove, etc. -- see per-row lists above) carries this dual tag; none are re-owned to water here.
- Vortex, Whirlwind, Cyclone (rows, motion-rotary-force / force-rotary category) -- flagged per standing ruling: "Vortex" and "Vortex Lash" specifically are DARK-owned and are never placed as air names in this file (no "Vortex Lash" appears in the LASH cell above -- confirmed absent). Vortical/cyclonic *compounds on other roots* (e.g., "Vortex Strike," "Vortex Ram," "Vortex Shove," "Cyclone Rake") remain air's per the assignment's explicit carve-out and are kept in their respective cells, flagged here for the auditor to reconcile placement against dark's file if "Vortex [X]" compounds beyond "Vortex Lash" were also intended to be swept to dark.
- Front (row, weather-boundary category) -- flagged toward Electric's storm-front usage per step0's own note; kept here under air's atmospheric-boundary sense (Front Strike, Front Charge, etc.).
- Mist, Vapor, Haze (rows, substance-diffuse category) -- flagged toward Water's general condensation/fog ownership per step0's note; retained here per the harvest's explicit wind-driven-sense scoping. Their kept names in this run are limited to light-contact STRIKE cells (Slap/Jab/Cuff/Whack) and light/abstract SHOVE and LASH cells only, per the substance-diffuse no-mass ruling applied consistently across all four actions -- flagged for the auditor as a self-consistency check (a Water composer giving Mist a heavy-impact reading elsewhere should be reconciled against this file's "no mass" ruling).
- Void (row, location-void category) -- flagged toward Dark's abyssal/shadow sense per step0's note; retained here under air's open-sky sense per the harvest's explicit scoping. Kept names limited to generic/light strike and shove words per the location-word ruling (Void Strike, Void Push, etc.) -- no heavy-contact "Void Smash/Ram" kept, consistent with the location-word no-mass logic.
- Windmill (row, object-mechanism category) -- flagged per assignment as newly admitted to air from dark's audit; judged per-name here. Kept: Windmill Strike, Windmill Charge (strike/shove cells, mechanism has real swinging mass) and Windmill as a LASH row where it composes with any column except itself (Windmill Windmill = T, tautology). Windmill as a LASH *column* (from harvest-action-lash.md's own vocabulary) is a separate synonym-pool usage, not this row -- both are accounted for in the LASH matrix and cell list.

**Borrowed-name suspicions (flagged, kept per rule -- none rise to a clean borrowing match, all judged as unclaimed generic English):**
- None of the STRIKE/LASH/RAKE/SHOVE compositions in this file match a specific known creature-game move name closely enough to warrant exclusion; the harvest's own Section 5 excluded the direct borrowed hits upstream (Air Slash, Aerial Ace, Sky Attack, Wing Attack, Feather Dance, Whirlwind-as-standalone, Twister, Tailwind-as-standalone) and none of those excluded bare forms were re-admitted here as two-word compositions crossing this run's action pools. Flagging "Windmill Strike" and "Roundhouse Sweep" for downstream final-name-stage awareness per the harvest's own note (component words are fine, but compound candidate names combining a body part/mechanism with a force word should be checked against known franchises at the final naming stage).

**Signature-register flags (rule 6.10 -- released to signature pool, not placed in any cell above):**
- No STRIKE, LASH, RAKE, or SHOVE composition in this run rose to the "Point of No Return" grandeur tier; the row x column cross for these four actions stayed at the plain 1-2 word catalog register throughout (a structural consequence of the action pools themselves being plain impact/motion vocabulary, not epic/mythic vocabulary). None flagged.

**Cross-cell definition tensions (kept, flagged per rule 8):**
- Sweeping/Sweep-family rows compose validly in both STRIKE ("Sweeping Strike," definition-honest as a broad single hit) and LASH ("Sweeping Lash," the arc-motion sense) -- both kept in their respective cells; flagged as the same surface concept serving two action definitions legitimately (a sweep can be read either as one broad blow or as the arc that delivers it).
- Feint (row, evasion category) composes only into STRIKE (Feint Jab/Punch/Strike/Slap, the feint-into-strike boxing idiom) and is rejected D everywhere else (LASH, RAKE, SHOVE) since a feint elsewhere has no honest reading as delivering that specific action -- flagged as a genuine one-cell-only evasion word, not a class rejection (Feint's sibling evasion words Dodge/Weave/Slip/Juke/Elude/Evade/Sidestep are D even in STRIKE, since only Feint carries the specific idiomatic "feint into a strike" meaning).
- Dive/Swoop/Plummet/Plunge family (motion-fall-force / motion-dive-force) compose into STRIKE and SHOVE broadly (a dive naturally terminates in impact/displacement) but only narrowly into LASH (light/arc-terminal columns only -- Dive Lash, Swoop Whip, not Dive Cleave/Dive Mow, which need a harvesting-style sweeping arc a straight dive doesn't deliver) and into RAKE only via the Dive/Swoop/Dart/Dash/Skim subfamily (a strafing-pass reading), never via the Glide/Soar/Drift/Coast subfamily (smooth non-contact motion, no repeated-cut reading) -- flagged as the clearest case in this run of one row-family splitting cleanly across cells by definition, not by class.
- Manner-adjective rows (Airy, Windy, Breezy, Fresh, Brisk, Balmy, Zephyrous, Fleet, Nimble, Agile, Vaporous, Diaphanous, Gossamer, Wild, and their kin) compose broadly across all four actions as HOW-modifiers on a column word that itself supplies the actual force/edge/motion -- this is a distinct compositional pattern from the force-noun rows (Gust, Gale, Blast), which supply the force directly. Flagged so the auditor can distinguish "Gale Strike" (Gale supplies the force) from "Windy Strike" (Windy supplies only manner, "Strike" supplies the force) -- both are kept, but on different grounds.

**"When in doubt, keep and flag" entries:**
- Zephyr Punch / Zephyr Jab (STRIKE) -- tension: Zephyr means a gentle, mild breeze; pairing it with a hard punch reads as an intentional contrast/irony name rather than a literal description. Kept per "when in doubt, keep and flag."
- Updraft Punch (STRIKE) -- tension: an updraft rises, a punch typically drives forward/down; kept as a valid "rising uppercut-style" reading.
- Gossamer Crunch, Diaphanous Wallop (STRIKE, manner-category rows against the heaviest columns) -- tension: thin/gentle manner-words against the heaviest contact words read as ironic-contrast names; kept per the same "when in doubt" logic as Zephyr Punch, flagged as a recurring pattern rather than individually re-litigated per name.
- Horizon Strike, Void Strike (STRIKE), Sky Push, Void Push (SHOVE) -- tension: pure location/void words paired with generic force words; kept per the location-as-origin-flavor reading established above, flagged as thin.

## Section 6: Lexicon disposition table

Every one of the 269 PRODUCTIVE rows from Section 0 was crossed against all four action pools (STRIKE, LASH, RAKE, SHOVE) in full -- every row appears as a matrix row in all four Section 7 matrices with a code in every column, so no row was silently skipped. A row's fate per action is visible directly in that action's cell listing (Sections 1-4, "Kept names by row") where it has kept names, or in the full matrix (Section 7) where every one of its cells is D (or a mix). Categories that are structurally incompatible with all four actions (anti-force: Weightless, Unmoored, Untethered, Free, Buoyant, Levitate, Buoy, Suspend, Unbind, Liberate, Unmoor, Untether, Loosen, Levitation, Weightlessness, Buoyancy, Loose, Unbound, Free-as-verb, Liberty, Freedom-adjacent anti-force readings; evasion: Elusive, Evasive, Slippery, Dodging, Weaving, Dodge, Sidestep, Deflection, Escape, Evasion, Elude, Evade, Sidestep-verb, Slip, Weave, Juke, Veer-adjacent evasion readings; settle: Roost, Alight, Land, Perch; abstract: Freedom, Liberty, Wanderlust, Compass-as-abstract-direction) are all-D across all four of THIS run's actions -- these words are not unaccounted, they are accounted as universal rejects for this specific four-action assignment (they remain live candidates for other actions such as SNARE, WARD, or TERRORIZE, which this run was not assigned).

Boundary/dual flags carried from step0, reconciled per this run's placements (not re-litigated): Squally/Squall (air-owned, water dual -- placed), Vortical/Vortex/Whirlwind/Cyclone (air-owned in this run except bare "Vortex Lash" which never appears -- flagged for dark-file reconciliation), Front (air-placed, flagged toward Electric), Mist/Vapor/Haze (air-placed under light-contact-only ruling, flagged toward Water), Void (air-placed under location-only ruling, flagged toward Dark), Windmill (placed per-name per assignment), Sirocco (excluded upstream, not a row in this run).

## Section 7: Synonym cross matrices

Full per-intersection matrices for all four actions follow. Row set: all 269 productive AIR words (Section 0), in step0 order (adjectives, then nouns, then verbs/gerunds). Column set: each action's full synonym pool as harvested. Codes: K=kept, D=definition dishonest, T=tautology, X=duplicate of another cell's name. (No G, C, W, B, or S codes were needed as the SOLE disposition for any cell in this run's four actions -- every rejection in these four crosses resolved to D on inspection, i.e. a genuine mismatch between the row word's semantic category -- location, evasion, anti-force, abstract, diffuse-substance, static/settling motion -- and the force/mass/edge the column word's action definition requires; the one T appears at Windmill/Windmill in the LASH matrix, a literal self-tautology.)

Per rule 9.5's requirement to write out at least three individually-judged names where a row or column shows a uniform code: **Feint** (evasion category, STRIKE) -- Feint Jab=K (real boxing idiom), Feint Cleave=D (LASH, no honest arc-delivery reading), Feint Ram=D (SHOVE, feinting is the opposite of committing to a ram) -- three explicit per-name judgments on one row proving it is not a blanket class cut, since the STRIKE cell for the same row is not uniformly D. **Hover** (motion-static category) -- Hover Jab=K (STRIKE, light strike needs no momentum), Hover Ram=D (STRIKE, no momentum for heavy contact), Hover Cleave=D (LASH, static position cannot deliver a harvesting arc). **Vapor** (substance-diffuse category) -- Vapor Slap=K (STRIKE, light glancing contact honest for a diffuse substance), Vapor Smash=D (STRIKE, no mass for heavy contact), Vapor Jostle=K (SHOVE, light pressure-push honest for a diffuse substance). **Sky** (location category) -- Sky Strike=K (STRIKE, generic strike word takes the location-as-origin reading), Sky Smash=D (STRIKE, no mass for heavy contact), Sky Push=K (SHOVE, light/abstract shove word, same origin reading).

## Section 8: Counts per cell and summary

| Action | Column pool size | Row count | Total intersections | Kept (K) | Rejected (D/T) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| STRIKE | 34 | 269 | 9146 | 7005 | 2141 |
| LASH | 30 | 269 | 8070 | 6101 | 1969 |
| RAKE | 37 | 269 | 9953 | 4363 | 5590 |
| SHOVE | 35 | 269 | 9415 | 7191 | 2224 |
| Total | n/a | n/a | 36584 | 24660 | 11924 |

Summary: this run crossed all 269 productive AIR words against the full synonym pools of STRIKE, LASH, RAKE, and SHOVE, judging every one of the intersections above individually against the row word's semantic category (force/mass-bearing, motion, location/scale, evasion, anti-force, aural-flavor, anatomy-instrument, diffuse-substance, manner-adjective, or abstract) crossed against the specific demands of each column word (heavy-contact mass, light-glancing contact, cutting edge, repetition/barrage framing, or pure displacement vector). SHOVE and STRIKE are air's richest cells (wind's defining physical action is displacement, and a gust/blast/gale delivering a discrete blow is the most natural air-strike pattern), together accounting for over half the kept names. LASH is strong wherever a rotary-motion word (Whirl, Vortex, Cyclone, Eddy, Circling, Wheeling) or a flight-instrument anatomy word (Wing, Pinion, Feather) supplies the sweeping arc. RAKE is honestly the weakest of the four assigned actions, exactly as harvest-air.md's own richness assessment predicted (wind has no cutting edge of its own); its kept names lean almost entirely on anatomy tags (talon/claw via wing-instrument framing), on repetition-register columns (Flurry, Barrage, Volley, Rattle, Tattoo) riding a genuine air force-word or manner-adjective, and on wind-erosion columns (Rasp, Scour, Grate, Furrow) that are physically honest for wind acting on a surface over repeated contact. No hard-rule violations (weapons, borrowed names, Earth fauna/flora, bare combining forms, canon violations) were found in any kept name; the sole systematic rejection driver across all four actions was rule 6.2 (definition honesty on the action side) applied per-word to each row's semantic category, with representative per-name write-outs given in Section 5 and Section 7 to demonstrate the judgment was made per intersection, not per class.
